~~~python
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d

# ---------------- TERO ----------------
def solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    comp, stack = {snk}, [snk]
    while stack:
        x = stack.pop()
        for yv in adj[x]:
            if yv not in comp:
                comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
    if src not in comp:
        raise ValueError("源与汇不连通")
    unk = [n for n in nodes if n in comp and n != snk]
    idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
    m = len(unk)
    A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
    b = [F(0)] * m
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
            continue
        for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
            if x in idx:
                A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                if yv in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
    b[idx[src]] = F(I)
    for col in range(m):
        piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
        A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
        for r in range(m):
            if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                for k in range(col, m):
                    A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                b[r] -= fct * b[col]
    p = {n: None for n in nodes}
    p[snk] = F(0)
    for n in unk:
        p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    p = solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
    Q = []
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or p[u] is None or p[v] is None:
            Q.append(F(0))
        else:
            Q.append(dd * (p[u] - p[v]))
    return p, Q

def f_tero2(q):
    return q * q / (1 + q * q)

def make_tero(nodes, edges, src, snk, I, h, gam, eps_rm, grid=None, f=f_tero2):
    """位 = 管段序号；d 为导率（D⁰ = 0 的申报）；活动 = 各管流量与结点压力；写入 h(f(|Q|) − γD)；
    post：网格化与移除阈（导率低于 eps_rm 置 0：退浅极限，派生关联改变，定理 18-9）。信号 s 为注入强度倍数（'I' 为常注入）。"""
    from jxs_core import Host
    pos = list(range(len(edges)))
    def read(d, r, s):
        D = [d[k] for k in pos]
        p, Q = flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
        return {"Q": tuple(Q), "p": tuple(p[n] for n in nodes)}
    def comps(d, y, s):
        return {k: [("ADAPT", h * (f(abs(y["Q"][k])) - gam * d[k]))] for k in pos if d[k] > 0}
    def post(dn, do):
        out = dict(dn)
        for k in pos:
            v = out[k]
            if grid is not None:
                v = F(round(v * grid), grid)
            out[k] = v if v >= eps_rm else F(0)
        return out
    return Host(pos, read, comps, post)

# ---------------- CHAIN：边沁的表示 ----------------
# 潜在位 X̂ = {0, 1, 2, 3}；存在变量 e_x ∈ {0,1} 与深度 d_x ∈ [0, CAP]；信号 'S' 向现存链的首个未满位注入 1/2；
# 位 x 满（d_x = CAP）且信号继续时，若 x+1 不存在则生成（e_{x+1} ← 1）——满后沿边生成新结构位（《场数理式》方程 10 的最小表示）。
CAP = F(1)

def chain_positions():
    return [("d", x) for x in range(4)] + [("e", x) for x in range(4)]

def chain_zero():
    z = {p: F(0) for p in chain_positions()}
    z[("e", 0)] = F(1)
    return z

def chain_read(d, r, s):
    present = [x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1]
    # 活动：信号 S 的通行深度（进入第几位）与各现存位的出流读数（d_x 越深出流越易：读数 = d_x）
    if s == "R":
        return {"out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}
    tgt = next((x for x in present if d[("d", x)] < CAP), None)
    return {"tgt": tgt, "out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}

def chain_comps(d, y, s):
    cs = {}
    if s == "S":
        tgt = y["tgt"]
        if tgt is not None:
            room = CAP - d[("d", tgt)]
            cs[("d", tgt)] = [("FILL", min(F(1, 2), room))]
        else:
            last = max(x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1)
            if last + 1 < 4:
                cs[("e", last + 1)] = [("BIRTH", F(1))]
    return cs

def make_chain():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(chain_positions(), chain_read, chain_comps)

# ---------------- LOOP：γ1 自环玩具（符号律） ----------------
# 单一 γ1 子集深度 s ∈ [0, 1]；MBON 驱动 m = 2(1 − s)，M 发放 ⇔ m ≥ θ_M；DAN 驱动 = c + u·[M 发放]，D 发放 ⇔ ≥ θ_D；
# 非强化 CS+ 拍：D 发放则写入 +A（压抑加深，夹限 1），否则只退浅 ×(1 − ρ)。
def loop_step(s, u, c=F(1, 2), thM=F(1), thD=F(1, 2), A=F(1, 4), rho=F(1, 10)):
    m = 2 * (1 - s)
    gM = 1 if m >= thM else 0
    gD = 1 if c + u * gM >= thD else 0
    s2 = (1 - rho) * s + (A if gD else 0)
    return min(F(1), s2), gM, gD

# ---------------- 一般边界的导率网络（定理 18-9（二）） ----------------
def components(nodes, edges, D):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    seen, out = set(), []
    for n in nodes:
        if n in seen:
            continue
        comp, stack = {n}, [n]
        while stack:
            x = stack.pop()
            for yv in adj[x]:
                if yv not in comp:
                    comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
        seen |= comp
        out.append(comp)
    return out

def solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    """导率网络在一般边界下的稳态：fixed = {结点: 定势}，inj = {结点: 注入}（只计自由结点）。
    逐连通分量求解：含定势结点的分量唯一可解；不含者须分量内注入之和为 0（守恒），否则 ValueError；
    不含定势结点的分量取其最小结点势为 0（流量与此规范无关）。"""
    p = {}
    for comp in components(nodes, edges, D):
        fx = [n for n in comp if n in fixed]
        if not fx:
            if sum(F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in comp) != 0:
                raise ValueError("分量源汇不守恒，稳态无解")
            ref = min(comp, key=str)
            known = {ref: F(0)}
        else:
            known = {n: F(fixed[n]) for n in fx}
        unk = sorted([n for n in comp if n not in known], key=str)
        idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
        m = len(unk)
        A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
        b = [F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in unk]
        for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
            if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
                continue
            for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
                if x in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                    if yv in idx:
                        A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
                    else:
                        b[idx[x]] += dd * known[yv]
        for col in range(m):
            piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
            A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
            for r in range(m):
                if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                    fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                    for k in range(col, m):
                        A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                    b[r] -= fct * b[col]
        p.update(known)
        for n in unk:
            p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    p = solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj)
    return [dd * (p[u] - p[v]) if dd > 0 else F(0) for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D)]

def removal_response(nodes, edges, D, fixed, k):
    """同一边界下的增量传递：去掉第 k 条边 e = (u, v) 后，定势结点增量保持为 0（齐次边界），
    自由结点中 u 注入单位流、v 抽出单位流（定势端的注入由边界吸收，不计）时各边的流量。"""
    u, v = edges[k]
    Dc = list(D); Dc[k] = F(0)
    inj = {}
    if u not in fixed:
        inj[u] = F(1)
    if v not in fixed:
        inj[v] = inj.get(v, F(0)) - 1
    return flows_bc(nodes, edges, Dc, {n: F(0) for n in fixed}, inj)
~~~

A.3　jxs\_run.py　运行入口：节 T1—T25

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录程序：运行入口。
运行：python3 jxs_run.py；任一项不符时退出码为 1。
地位：有限核验、构造见证与反例；全称结论以正文定理的证明为准。
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product, permutations
from pathlib import Path
HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import jxs_core as C  # noqa: E402
import jxs_models as M  # noqa: E402
RES = []

def check(tag, cond, note=""):
    RES.append(bool(cond))
    print(("  [通过] " if cond else "  [不符] ") + tag + ("：" + note if note else ""))

def fs(x):
    x = F(x)
    return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else "%d/%d" % (x.numerator, x.denominator)

def main():
    print("《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录程序运行输出")
    print("=" * 60)
    fly = M.make_fly()
    # ---------------- T1 五层：势、活动、迹应与残留的分层（定理 18-1） ----------------
    print("T1　五层：势、活动、迹应与残留的分层（定理 18-1）")
    Xn = M.fly_zero(); Xn[(2, "g1")] = Xn[(3, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    Yn = M.fly_zero(); Yn[(0, "g1")] = Yn[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    yXn, dXn, _, _ = C.step(fly, Xn, None, "A")
    yYn, dYn, _, _ = C.step(fly, Yn, None, "A")
    check("T1a 同一深度多重集、落位不同：同一信号 A 下活动不同（MBON-γ1(A) = %s 对 %s），α3 迹应的后继不同（d(0,a3) = %s 对 %s）" %
          (fs(yXn["MBON_g1"]), fs(yYn["MBON_g1"]), fs(dXn[(0, "a3")]), fs(dYn[(0, "a3")])),
          sorted(Xn.values()) == sorted(Yn.values()) and yXn != yYn and dXn[(0, "a3")] != dYn[(0, "a3")])
    X0 = M.fly_zero(); X0[(0, "g1")] = F(3, 4); X0[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2); X0[(2, "a3")] = F(1, 4)
    Tn = M.fly_zero(); Tn[(0, "g1")] = F(1, 2); Tn[(2, "a3")] = F(1, 4)
    check("T1b 同一迹应集合、不同信号：活动不同（势在不同信号处的值），读出本身不改迹应",
          M.fly_read(Tn, None, "A") != M.fly_read(Tn, None, "B") and Tn == dict(Tn))
    p1n, p1e = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4], [(0, 1), (1, 3), (0, 2), (2, 3), (1, 4)]
    DX, DY = [F(2), F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1)], [F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1), F(2)]
    _, QXn = M.flows(p1n, p1e, DX, 0, 3, 1)
    _, QYn = M.flows(p1n, p1e, DY, 0, 3, 1)
    check("T1c 流网（含悬端支管 (1,4)）：同一导率多重集、落位不同，同一注入下流量分配不同：%s 对 %s；悬端管无流" % ([fs(q) for q in QXn], [fs(q) for q in QYn]),
          sorted(DX) == sorted(DY) and QXn != QYn and QYn[4] == 0)
    p4n, p4e = [0, 1, 2], [(0, 1), (0, 2)]
    DX4, DY4 = [F(1), F(1)], [F(1), F(3)]
    pXa, QXa = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DX4, 0, 1, 1)
    pYa, QYa = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DY4, 0, 1, 1)
    pXb, _ = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DX4, 2, 1, 1)
    pYb, _ = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DY4, 2, 1, 1)
    check("T1d 实际信号下全活动相同而势不同：实际注入（源 0）下两态流量与压力相同；以结点 2 为探针源时压差 %s 对 %s" %
          (fs(pXb[2]), fs(pYb[2])), QXa == QYa and pXa == pYa and pXb[2] != pYb[2])
    th1 = F(1)
    phi2 = lambda d, rr, sg: 1 if d + rr + sg >= th1 else 0
    P2 = lambda rr, y: F(1, 2) * rr + F(1, 4) * y
    sq0, sq1, r0, r1 = [], [], F(0), F(3, 4)
    for _ in range(4):
        y0, y1 = phi2(F(1, 2), r0, F(0)), phi2(F(1, 2), r1, F(0))
        sq0.append(y0); sq1.append(y1)
        r0, r1 = P2(r0, y0), P2(r1, y1)
    check("T1e 残留进入运行语义：同 d = 1/2、同输入 0，r = 0 与 3/4 的活动序列 %s 对 %s，势须以 (d, r) 为论元" % (sq0, sq1),
          sq0 != sq1 and phi2(F(1, 2), F(0), F(0)) != phi2(F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(0)))
    inv_ok = True
    for i_d in range(8):
        for i_s in range(8):
            d_, sg, rr = F(i_d, 8), F(i_s, 8), F(0)
            if d_ + sg >= 1:
                continue
            for _ in range(6):
                rr = P2(rr, phi2(d_, rr, sg))
                if rr != 0:
                    inv_ok = False
    check("T1f r 可唯一消元的不变域 {r = 0，d + s < 1，输入恒为 s}：网格上 6 拍内 r 恒为 0；域外 d = s = 1/2 一步后 r = %s" %
          fs(P2(F(0), phi2(F(1, 2), F(0), F(1, 2)))), inv_ok and P2(F(0), phi2(F(1, 2), F(0), F(1, 2))) != 0)
    G1n = M.fly_zero(); G1n[(0, "g1")] = G1n[(1, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    _, dG1, _, _ = C.step(fly, G1n, None, "A")
    _, dG0, _, _ = C.step(fly, G1n, None, "R")
    check("T1g 探针即写入：写入门开时以 A 读势改变 α3 迹应（d(0,a3)：0 → %s）；静置不读不写" % fs(dG1[(0, "a3")]),
          dG1[(0, "a3")] != 0 and dG0[(0, "a3")] == 0)
    # ---------------- T2 五层与同一（定理 18-1、18-2） ----------------
    print("T2　改名等变与两套同一（定理 18-2；含 U、V 归纳的有限对拍）")
    X = M.fly_zero(); X[(0, "g1")] = X[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    Y = M.fly_zero(); Y[(2, "g1")] = Y[(3, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    check("T2a 同一深度多重集、落位不同：A 处势不同", C.potential(fly, X, None, "A") != C.potential(fly, Y, None, "A"))
    U = M.fly_zero(); U[(0, "g1")] = F(1)
    V = M.fly_zero(); V[(0, "g1")] = V[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    same, n = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (U, None), (V, None), "ABR", 6)
    check("T2b U、V 在全部 %d 条长度 ≤ 6 的 ABR 史上精确动力同一（全称：定理 18-2 归纳）" % n, same and n == 1093)
    same_c, _ = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (U, None), (V, None), "ABCR", 1)
    check("T2c 加入交叠气味 C 即可分（动力同一相对输入协议）", not same_c)
    def g_apply(pi, tau, d):
        return {(pi[i], tau[c]): v for (i, c), v in d.items()}
    G4 = []
    for pk in permutations(M.KC):
        pi = dict(zip(M.KC, pk))
        for so in permutations("ABC"):
            sig = dict(zip("ABC", so)); sig["R"] = "R"
            if all(sorted(pi[i] for i in M.ODOR[s]) == sorted(M.ODOR[sig[s]]) for s in "ABC"):
                for tau in ({c: c for c in M.COMP}, {"g1": "g1", "a3": "a3", "P": "N", "N": "P"}):
                    G4.append((pi, sig, tau))
    ok = True
    for pi, sig, tau in G4:
        for L in range(4):
            for h in product("ABCR", repeat=L):
                y1, _, _ = C.run(fly, X0, None, h)
                y2, _, _ = C.run(fly, g_apply(pi, tau, X0), None, tuple(sig[s] for s in h))
                ren = [{("MBON_" + tau[k[5:]] if k.startswith("MBON_") else k): v for k, v in y.items()} for y in y1]
                if ren != y2:
                    ok = False
    check("T2d 申报改名子群 G4（|G4| = %d）逐元素在长度 ≤ 3 的全部史上等变" % len(G4), ok and len(G4) == 4)
    def in_g4(d1, d2):
        return any(g_apply(pi, tau, d1) == d2 for pi, sig, tau in G4)
    def acts(d, h):
        return C.run(fly, d, None, h)[0]
    def ren_same(d1, d2, L, alpha):
        for pi, sig, tau in G4:
            good = True
            for n_ in range(1, L + 1):
                for h in product(alpha, repeat=n_):
                    y1 = acts(d1, h)
                    y2 = acts(d2, tuple(sig[x] for x in h))
                    ren = [{("MBON_" + tau[k[5:]] if k.startswith("MBON_") else k): v for k, v in y.items()} for y in y1]
                    if ren != y2:
                        good = False; break
                if not good:
                    break
            if good:
                return True
        return False
    Xo = M.fly_zero(); Xo[(0, "g1")] = Xo[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    PIo = {0: 3, 1: 2, 2: 1, 3: 0}
    Yo = {(PIo[i], c): v for (i, c), v in Xo.items()}
    check("T2e 跨约定：X 与其 (0 3)(1 2) 像 Y 改名同一，固定标签 A 处势不同（MBON-γ1(A) = %s 对 %s），按 σ 改名后势相同；精确动力同一不成立（长度 ≤ 2），改名动力同一成立（长度 ≤ 3）" %
          (fs(M.fly_read(Xo, None, "A")["MBON_g1"]), fs(M.fly_read(Yo, None, "A")["MBON_g1"])),
          in_g4(Xo, Yo) and M.fly_read(Xo, None, "A") != M.fly_read(Yo, None, "A") and M.fly_read(Xo, None, "A") == M.fly_read(Yo, None, "B")
          and not C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (Xo, None), (Yo, None), "ABCR", 2)[0] and ren_same(Xo, Yo, 3, "ABCR"))
    check("T2f 精确约定：U ≠ V 而精确动力同一（T2b），且 U、V 元素值多重集不同，不在任何改名轨道上", U != V and not in_g4(U, V))
    Gu = M.fly_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Gu[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    Ua = dict(Gu); Ua[(0, "a3")] = F(1)
    Va = dict(Gu); Va[(0, "a3")] = Va[(1, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    ya1, ua1, _, _ = C.step(fly, Ua, None, "A")
    yb1, va1, _, _ = C.step(fly, Va, None, "A")
    ya2 = M.fly_read(ua1, None, "A"); yb2 = M.fly_read(va1, None, "A")
    check("T2g Ua、Va（α3 深度 1、0 对各 1/2）：首次呈现 A 全活动相同，第二次因夹限而不同（MBON-α3(A) = %s 对 %s）：当前势同而非动力同一" %
          (fs(ya2["MBON_a3"]), fs(yb2["MBON_a3"])), ya1 == yb1 and ya2 != yb2)
    now_ren = any(all({("MBON_" + tau[k[5:]] if k.startswith("MBON_") else k): v for k, v in M.fly_read(Ua, None, x).items()}
                      == M.fly_read(Va, None, sig[x]) for x in "AB") for pi, sig, tau in G4)
    check("T2h 改名约定（信号类 {A, B, R}）：Ua、Va 改名当前势同，而 G4 中每个 g 都在长度 ≤ 2 内把二者分开；信号类含交叠气味 C 时当前即可分",
          now_ren and not ren_same(Ua, Va, 2, "ABR") and M.fly_read(Ua, None, "C") != M.fly_read(Va, None, "C"))
    TAUS = {"g1": "a3", "a3": "g1", "P": "P", "N": "N"}
    Xs = M.fly_zero(); Xs[(0, "g1")] = Xs[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    Xt = {(i, TAUS[c]): v for (i, c), v in Xs.items()}
    check("T2i γ1↔α3 互换保持 KC×区室连接图，却不在 G4 中，且两态在 A 下全活动不同（PPL1-α3(A) = %s 对 %s）：连接图同构不足以给出组织同一" %
          (fs(M.fly_read(Xs, None, "A")["PPL1_a3"]), fs(M.fly_read(Xt, None, "A")["PPL1_a3"])),
          {(i, TAUS[c]) for (i, c) in M.FLY_POS} == set(M.FLY_POS) and all(t != TAUS for _, _, t in G4)
          and M.fly_read(Xs, None, "A") != M.fly_read(Xt, None, "A"))
    # ---------------- T3 分辨率语义（D18-8） ----------------
    print("T3　两套分辨率语义的四种情形（D18-8）")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    cases = [("单个阈下分项", [F(1, 20)]), ("多个阈下同号", [F(1, 20)] * 3), ("正负抵消", [F(1, 5), F(-1, 5)])]
    for name, cs in cases:
        host = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda cs_: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p%d" % k, c) for k, c in enumerate(cs_)]}))(cs))
        _, dr, _, rr = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
        _, db, _, rb = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "booked")
        check("T3 %s：原机制 Aff⁰ ⊆ Act⁰；入账 Aff⁰ ⊆ Act^ε" % name, rr["Aff0"] <= rr["Act0"] and rb["Aff0"] <= rb["Acte"])
    host = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p", F(1, 20))]})
    _, _, _, rr = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps)
    check("T3x 原机制下 Aff⁰ ⊄ Act^ε（单个阈下分项）", rr["Aff0"] - rr["Acte"] == {"x"})
    _, _, _, r0 = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", F(0))
    check("T3w ε = 0 时 Act^ε = Act⁰、Aff^ε = Aff⁰", r0["Acte"] == r0["Act0"] and r0["Affe"] == r0["Aff0"])
    # ---------------- T4 回读定理 18-4 ----------------
    print("T4　回读定理 18-4：两种历史条件读法、嵌入〔封二〕F 层")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    grid = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(1, 2), F(1)]
    vals = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(1, 2), F(1)]
    incl, strict = True, False
    count = 0
    for k in [F(0), F(1, 20), F(1, 4), F(1), F(-1, 2)]:
        for b in [F(0), F(1, 3)]:
            for s0 in grid:
                for s1 in grid:
                    for law in [("lin", b, k, ("t0",)), ("lin", b, k, ("t0", "t1")), ("bil", b, k, "t0", "t1")]:
                        s = {"t0": s0, "t1": s1}
                        formed = {"t0", "t1"}
                        hf = C.histcond_F(law, s, ["t0", "t1"], eps, formed)
                        hd = C.histcond_diff(law, s, ["t0", "t1"], eps, formed, {"t0": vals, "t1": vals})
                        count += 1
                        if hd == 1 and hf == 0:
                            incl = False
                        if hf == 1 and hd == 0:
                            strict = True
    check("T4a 差异读法（〔十七〕定义 6′ 的数值部分）⇒ 函数支持读法（〔封二〕D18），网格 %d 例无反例" % count, incl)
    check("T4b 逆不成立：存在函数支持读法 1 而差异读法 0 的例（小系数）", strict)
    law = ("lin", F(0), F(1, 20), ("t0",))
    s = {"t0": F(1)}
    check("T4c 具体例：c = (1/20)·d(t0)，d(t0) = 1，ε = 1/10：F 层 HistCond = 1，差异读法 = 0（|Δ| ≤ 1/20）",
          C.histcond_F(law, s, ["t0"], eps, {"t0"}) == 1 and C.histcond_diff(law, s, ["t0"], eps, {"t0"}, {"t0": vals}) == 0)
    # 嵌入：原机制与有效入账（〔封二〕4.2）轨迹相同 ⇔ 每拍每位阈下非零分项之和为零
    ok = True
    for cs in ([F(1, 5)], [F(1, 20)], [F(1, 20), F(-1, 20)], [F(1, 20), F(1, 5)], [F(1, 5), F(-1, 5)], [F(1, 20), F(1, 20), F(-1, 10)]):
        host = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda cs_: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p%d" % k, c) for k, c in enumerate(cs_)]}))(cs))
        _, dr, _, _ = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
        _, db, _, _ = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "booked")
        sub = sum(c for c in cs if 0 < abs(c) <= eps)
        if (dr == db) != (sub == 0):
            ok = False
    check("T4d 原机制与有效入账一拍相同 ⇔ 阈下非零分项之和为零（六例）", ok)
    # 果蝇 α3 写入（去夹限）编译为线性律 c = −ηa + ηb·Σ_{K(s)} d_γ1
    ok = True
    for v0 in grid:
        for v1 in grid:
            dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(0, "g1")] = v0; dd[(1, "g1")] = v1
            y = M.fly_read(dd, None, "A")
            c_sys = [c for p, c in M.fly_comps(dd, y, "A")[(0, "a3")] if p == "W_a3"][0]
            c_lin = C.f_component(("lin", -ETA_A(), ETA_B(), ((0, "g1"), (1, "g1"))), dd)
            if c_sys != c_lin:
                ok = False
    check("T4e 果蝇 α3 写入（活动中介支）在去夹限时编译为〔封二〕线性律：c = −ηa + ηb·Σ_{K(A)} d_γ1（网格 16 例）", ok)
    y = M.fly_read(M.fly_zero(), None, "A")
    dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(0, "a3")] = F(1)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        dd[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    y = M.fly_read(dd, None, "A")
    _, dn, _, rec = C.step(fly, dd, None, "A")
    raw_c = [c for p, c in rec["comps"][(0, "a3")]][0]
    check("T4f 夹限使后态 ≠ 前态 + 分项（d(0,a3)：1 + %s → 1），不在〔封二〕F 层律类内，须入 R 层（D9 联合读法或分列 CLIP）" % fs(raw_c),
          dn[(0, "a3")] == 1 and raw_c > 0)
    # ---------------- T5 局部环路增益（定理 18-5） ----------------
    print("T5　势的局部承担与环路增益（定理 18-5）")
    gam, h = F(11, 50), F(1, 2)
    for name, g, expect in (("R_th = 1/4", F(4), "消失"), ("R_th = 4", F(1, 4), "留存")):
        d0, q0 = F(1), F(1, 2)
        I0 = q0 * (g + d0) / d0
        R = 1 / g
        V = I0 / g
        # 一维映射 d ↦ d + h(f(dV/(1+dR)) − γd)：在 d0 处的环路增益 L = f′(q)·∂q/∂d
        q = d0 * V / (1 + d0 * R)
        fprime = 2 * q / (1 + q * q) ** 2
        dqdd = V / (1 + d0 * R) ** 2
        Lg = fprime * dqdd
        dd, traj = d0, []
        for _ in range(80):
            qq = dd * V / (1 + dd * R) if dd > 0 else F(0)
            dd = F(round((dd + h * (M.f_tero2(qq) - gam * dd)) * 256), 256) if dd > 0 else F(0)
            dd = dd if dd >= F(1, 32) else F(0)
            traj.append(dd)
        fate = "消失" if traj[-1] == 0 else "留存"
        check("T5 %s：环路增益 L = f′·∂Q/∂D = %s，%s γ = %s；80 拍后%s" % (name, fs(Lg), ">" if Lg > gam else "<", fs(gam), fate),
              fate == expect and ((Lg > gam) == (expect == "消失")))
    # 符号律：γ1 自环玩具
    s_neg, s_pos = F(3, 4), F(3, 4)
    seq_neg, seq_pos = [], []
    for _ in range(30):
        s_neg, _, gd1 = M.loop_step(s_neg, F(-1, 4), c=F(1, 2))
        s_pos, _, gd2 = M.loop_step(s_pos, F(1, 4), c=F(1, 4))
        seq_neg.append(s_neg); seq_pos.append(s_pos)
    check("T5c 符号律（正环）：u < 0 时深态保持在 1/2 之上（续写）", all(v > F(1, 2) for v in seq_neg))
    check("T5d 符号律（负环）：u > 0 时深度回落并被限于 1/2 附近（自限：此后 ≤ 1/2 + A）",
          min(seq_pos) <= F(1, 2) and all(v <= F(1, 2) + F(1, 4) for v in seq_pos[10:]))
    # ---------------- T6 等效承担（定理 18-6） ----------------
    print("T6　等效承担：线性汇聚的本位响应为仿射，导率网络的为分式线性")
    ok = True
    for rest in grid:
        dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(1, "a3")] = rest
        ys = [M.fly_read(dict(dd, **{}), None, "A") for _ in [0]]
        vals_m = []
        for own in [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]:
            e = dict(dd); e[(0, "a3")] = own
            vals_m.append(M.fly_read(e, None, "A")["MBON_a3"])
        if not (vals_m[0] - vals_m[1] == vals_m[1] - vals_m[2] == F(1, 2) and vals_m[0] == 2 - rest):
            ok = False
    check("T6a MBON-α3(A) 对本位 d(0,a3) 为仿射 (2 − rest) − d_own，其余集合只经截距 rest 承担（网格 4 例）", ok)
    nodes5 = [0, 1, 2, 3]
    edges5 = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D0 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    k = 4
    Dm = list(D0); Dm[k] = F(0)
    p, _ = M.flows(nodes5, edges5, Dm, 0, 3, 1)
    Vt = p[1] - p[2]
    pr = M.solve(nodes5, edges5, Dm, 1, 2, 1)
    Rt = pr[1] - pr[2]
    ok = True
    for own in [F(1, 4), F(1), F(3)]:
        Dv = list(D0); Dv[k] = own
        _, Q = M.flows(nodes5, edges5, Dv, 0, 3, 1)
        if Q[k] != own * Vt / (1 + own * Rt):
            ok = False
    check("T6b 导率网络：管 (1,2) 流量 = d·V/(1 + dR)，V = %s、R = %s 由其余集合承担" % (fs(Vt), fs(Rt)), ok)
    # ---------------- T7 隐变（定理 18-7） ----------------
    print("T7　隐变的闭环判据（定理 18-7）")
    check("T7a 部分读数：A_cl = [[1,1],[0,1]]、C = [1,0]、Δ = (0,1) 判显现",
          not C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1), F(1)], [F(0), F(1)]], [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)]))
    check("T7b 平行对立等率永隐、不等率显现",
          C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(4, 5), 0], [0, F(4, 5)]], [[F(-2), F(2)]], [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]) and
          not C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(19, 20), 0], [0, F(4, 5)]], [[F(-2), F(2)]], [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]))
    # ---------------- T8 巩固态（定理 18-8） ----------------
    print("T8　写入律被历史改写：巩固态作为律层迹应（定理 18-8）")
    flyc = M.make_flyc()
    base = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        base[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    P0 = dict(base); P1 = dict(base)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        P1[("c", i)] = F(1)
    check("T8a 同 d、异巩固态 c：一切信号下当前全活动相同（势零空间）",
          all(M.flyc_read(P0, None, s) == M.flyc_read(P1, None, s) for s in "ABC"))
    y0, _, _ = C.run(flyc, P0, None, ("R",) * 3 + ("A",))
    y1, _, _ = C.run(flyc, P1, None, ("R",) * 3 + ("A",))
    check("T8b 静置 3 拍后呈现 A：保留比不同使 MBON-α3(A) 不同（经已有深度显现）：%s 对 %s" %
          (fs(y0[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(y1[-1]["MBON_a3"])), y0[-1] != y1[-1])
    Z0 = M.flyc_zero(); Z1 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Z1[("c", i)] = F(1)
    same, n = C.exact_dyn_same(flyc, (Z0, None), (Z1, None), "BR", 5)
    check("T8c 深度为零时，只含 B 与静置的 %d 条史上巩固态差永隐（无新写入落到这些位）" % n, same)
    G1 = dict(Z0); G2 = dict(Z1)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        G1[(i, "g1")] = G2[(i, "g1")] = F(13, 32)
    ya, da, _ = C.run(flyc, G1, None, ("A", "R", "R", "R", "A"))
    yb, db, _ = C.run(flyc, G2, None, ("A", "R", "R", "R", "A"))
    check("T8d 阈下巩固的弱写入之后显现：PPL1-α3(A) = 1/16（> 0 而 ≤ θ_c），写入 α3 而不置巩固态；静置后第二次呈现 A 读数不同：%s 对 %s" %
          (fs(ya[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(yb[-1]["MBON_a3"])), ya[-1] != yb[-1] and da[("c", 0)] == 0)
    H1 = dict(Z0); H2 = dict(Z1)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        H1[(i, "g1")] = H2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    same, n = C.exact_dyn_same(flyc, (H1, None), (H2, None), "ABR", 4)
    check("T8g 强写入（PPL1-α3 > θ_c）同时置巩固态，覆盖先前的巩固态差：长度 ≤ 4 的 %d 条 ABR 史上读数逐拍相同（有限核验；覆盖后完整态相同见 T15d，未覆盖的晚显见 T15e）" % n, same)
    # 历史控巩固与钟控巩固：阻断 DAN 的区分
    T0 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        T0[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); T0[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    hist = ("A", "R", "R", "R", "R", "A")
    hb_host = M.make_flyc(block_dan=True)
    hb_ys, hb_d, _ = C.run(hb_host, T0, None, hist)
    hn_ys, hn_d, _ = C.run(M.make_flyc(), T0, None, hist)
    cb_ys, cb_d = M.flyc_clock_run(dict(T0), hist, 1, block_dan=True)
    cn_ys, cn_d = M.flyc_clock_run(dict(T0), hist, 1, block_dan=False)
    # 两臂同一训练史、同一阻断：阻断在两种假设下都去除 α3 的直写分项（排除「阻断只因去除直写而改变保留」的解释）
    cs_h = hb_host.comps(T0, M.flyc_read(T0, None, "A"), "A")
    cs_c = M.make_flyc(clock=1, block_dan=True).comps(T0, M.flyc_read(T0, None, "A"), "A")
    no_direct = all(p_[0] != "W_a3" for lst in list(cs_h.values()) + list(cs_c.values()) for p_ in lst)
    check("T8e 两臂（历史控、钟控）同一训练与读出、同一阻断，阻断在两臂都去除 α3 直写分项", no_direct)
    check("T8f 阻断臂：历史控 c 仍为 0、钟控 c 置 1；两臂 α3 末态与末拍读数不同（差只经 c）：MBON-α3(A) %s 对 %s" %
          (fs(hb_ys[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(cb_ys[-1]["MBON_a3"])),
          hb_d[("c", 0)] == 0 and cb_d[("c", 0)] == 1 and hb_d[(0, "a3")] < cb_d[(0, "a3")] and hb_ys[-1] != cb_ys[-1])
    check("T8h 未阻断：两种假设下 c 都置 1（不阻断不能区分）", hn_d[("c", 0)] == cn_d[("c", 0)] == 1)
    # 生效时刻：c 在拍前态读取，本拍写入的新 c 自下一拍起生效
    Tq = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Tq[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); Tq[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    _, d1q, _, recq = C.step(M.make_flyc(), Tq, None, "A")
    rel = [c for p_, c in recq["comps"][(0, "a3")] if p_ == "RELAX_a3"][0]
    check("T8i 生效时刻：写入 c 的同一拍，α3 弛豫仍按旧 c（ρ = 1/2）计：%s；新 c 自下一拍起生效" % fs(rel),
          rel == -(1 - M.RHO[0]) * Tq[(0, "a3")] and d1q[("c", 0)] == 1)
    # 饱和读出可使律层造成的状态差不可见
    sat = lambda d: min(d, F(1, 2))
    check("T8j 律层差造成深度 1 与 3/4，饱和读出 min(d, 1/2) 两态均为 1/2：状态差不显现", sat(F(1)) == sat(F(3, 4)) == F(1, 2))
    # ---------------- T9 组织关系的变化（定理 18-9） ----------------
    print("T9　连接、交叠与子集关系的变化（定理 18-9）")
    dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(1, "g1")] = F(1)
    keff = lambda d, s, c: {i for i in M.ODOR[s] if d[(i, c)] < 1}
    ov0 = keff(M.fly_zero(), "A", "g1") & keff(M.fly_zero(), "C", "g1")
    ov1 = keff(dd, "A", "g1") & keff(dd, "C", "g1")
    check("T9a 有效交叠由深度派生：γ1 位 (1,g1) 压抑至极限后，A 与 C 在 γ1 的有效交叠由 {1} 变为 ∅", ov0 == {1} and ov1 == set())
    check("T9b 交叠改变改变势：C 处 MBON-γ1 由 2 变为 1",
          M.fly_read(M.fly_zero(), None, "C")["MBON_g1"] == 2 and M.fly_read(dd, None, "C")["MBON_g1"] == 1)
    nodes4 = [0, 1, 2, 3]
    edges4 = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D4 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    t4 = M.make_tero(nodes4, edges4, 0, 3, F(1), F(1, 2), F(1), F(0))
    d4 = {k: D4[k] for k in range(5)}
    d4c = dict(d4); d4c[0] = F(0)
    y4, y4c = t4.read(d4, None, "I"), t4.read(d4c, None, "I")
    check("T9c 导率网络：管段达退浅极限（导率 0）即派生关联改变，非邻接管 (2,3) 流量 %s → %s" %
          (fs(y4["Q"][3]), fs(y4c["Q"][3])), y4["Q"][3] != y4c["Q"][3])
    ch = M.make_chain()
    dch = M.chain_zero()
    hist_present = []
    for t in range(7):
        y, dch, _, rec = C.step(ch, dch, None, "S")
        hist_present.append(sum(1 for x in range(4) if dch[("e", x)] == 1))
    check("T9d 边沁的最小表示：首位满后信号继续则沿边生成新位，现存位数 %s" % hist_present,
          hist_present[0] == 1 and hist_present[-1] > 1 and all(hist_present[i] <= hist_present[i + 1] for i in range(6)))
    check("T9e 生成后势改变：同一信号 S 的通行目标由无位可入变为新位",
          ch.read(dch, None, "S")["tgt"] is not None)
    # ---------------- T10 回读：路径起点、读取认证、联合对照与 F 层入账 ----------------
    print("T10　回读：路径起点、读取认证、联合对照与 F 层入账（定理 18-4（二）（四））")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    law = ("lin", F(1), F(1, 20), ("d",))
    s1 = {"d": F(1)}
    ok_path = lambda S: 1
    check("T10a 有效写入上的读法分离：c = 1 + d/20，d = 1，ε = 1/10：实际分项 21/20 > ε；函数支持（路径自 Avail 出发、读取认证 1）已立；联合对照的最大差 1/20，数值差未越阈",
          C.f_component(law, s1) == F(21, 20) and C.histcond_F_full(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, ok_path) == 1 and
          C.histcond_diff_joint(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, {"d": [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]}) == "数值差未越阈")
    only_other = lambda S: 1 if S == frozenset({"z"}) else 0
    check("T10b 路径须自真正的 Avail_act 起点集出发：只有自非可用位 z 的路径时 HistCond_F = 0",
          C.histcond_F_full(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, only_other) == 0)
    check("T10c 读取认证 ⊥ 时 HistCond_F = ⊥（不升为 1）", C.histcond_F_full(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, ok_path, "⊥") == "⊥")
    law6 = ("lin", F(1), F(3, 50), ("a", "b"))
    st6 = {"a": F(1), "b": F(1)}
    check("T10d 联合对照：单设子协议无见证（各 3/50），联合设定差 3/25 > ε，数值差越阈",
          C.histcond_diff(law6, st6, ["a", "b"], eps, {"a", "b"}, {"a": [F(0)], "b": [F(0)]}) == 0 and
          C.histcond_diff_joint(law6, st6, ["a", "b"], eps, {"a", "b"}, {"a": [F(0)], "b": [F(0)]}) == "数值差越阈")
    r_tri = C.readback_hist(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, {"d": [F(0), F(1)]}, ok_path)
    check("T10e 两列原始判断：%s / %s" % r_tri, r_tri == ("函数支持已立", "数值差未越阈"))
    cases_ce = [[(F(1), 0)], [(F(1), 1)], [(F(1, 20), 1), (F(-1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 5), 1), (F(1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 5), 0), (F(1, 5), 1)]]
    gen_ok = all((C.raw_update(cs) == C.f_layer_update(cs, eps)) ==
                 (sum(c for c, _ in cs) == sum(c for c, ce in cs if abs(c) > eps and ce == 1)) for cs in cases_ce)
    check("T10f F 层入账计 cert_eff：常数分项 +1、cert_eff = 0 时阈下和为零而原机制与 F 层不同",
          C.raw_update(cases_ce[0]) != C.f_layer_update(cases_ce[0], eps))
    check("T10g 一般条件「同前态下两边入账总量相等」与一拍相同等价（五例）", gen_ok)
    print("T11　|F′| = 1 与两种干预（定理 18-5（二）（五））")
    def iterate(fn, u, n):
        out = [u]
        for _ in range(n):
            u = fn(u); out.append(u)
        return out
    a1 = iterate(lambda u: u - u ** 3, F(1, 4), 5)
    a2 = iterate(lambda u: u + u ** 3, F(1, 4), 5)
    check("T11a F′ = 1 的两例：u ↦ u − u³ 自 1/4 起绝对值严格减，u ↦ u + u³ 严格增：|F′| = 1 时待高阶",
          all(abs(a1[k + 1]) < abs(a1[k]) for k in range(5)) and all(abs(a2[k + 1]) > abs(a2[k]) for k in range(5)))
    phi_U = lambda dV: 2 - dV
    wU = lambda dU, y: dU + F(7)
    check("T11b 活动干预非零而后态干预为零：∂Φ_U/∂d_V = −1，写入对活动为常数，J_UV = 0",
          phi_U(F(1)) != phi_U(F(0)) and wU(F(1, 2), phi_U(F(1))) == wU(F(1, 2), phi_U(F(0))))
    print("T12　移除的远处效应（定理 18-9（二）（甲））")
    nodes4 = [0, 1, 2, 3]
    edges4 = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D4 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    _, Qo = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, D4, 0, 3, 1)
    Dc = list(D4); Dc[0] = F(0)
    _, Qn = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, Dc, 0, 3, 1)
    pT = M.solve(nodes4, edges4, Dc, 0, 1, 1)
    Tx = [dd_ * (pT[u] - pT[v]) if dd_ > 0 else F(0) for (u, v), dd_ in zip(edges4, Dc)]
    check("T12a 移除管段 e = (0,1) 引起的流量改变 ΔQ_x = Q_e·T_x(u→v)（T 为去掉 e 后自 u 注入单位流、自 v 抽出时 x 的流量），其余四管逐一成立",
          all(Qn[k] - Qo[k] == Qo[0] * Tx[k] for k in range(1, 5)))
    Db = [F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1)]
    _, Qb = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, Db, 0, 3, 1)
    Dbc = list(Db); Dbc[4] = F(0)
    _, Qbc = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, Dbc, 0, 3, 1)
    check("T12b 网络条件：平衡桥的桥管流量为 0，移除它不改变任何远处流量（移除本身不推出远处改变）",
          Qb[4] == 0 and Qb[:4] == Qbc[:4])
    print("T13　来源—身份记录（D18-2、D18-5）")
    def run_rec(d, hist, g=None):
        rec = []
        for t, sym in enumerate(hist):
            _, d, _, rr = C.step(fly, d, None, sym)
            for x, lst in rr["comps"].items():
                for pid, c in lst:
                    if c != 0 and pid == "W_a3":
                        rec.append((t, x, pid))
        return d, rec
    Xa = M.fly_zero(); Xa[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 8)
    Xb = M.fly_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Xb[(i, "g1")] = F(5, 8)
    dA, recA = run_rec(dict(Xa), ())
    pre = M.fly_zero(); pre[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 8)
    check("T13a 数学完整态相同（同 d、r）可来自不同形成记录：申报初值与经写入形成须由记录区分，记录不由数值态推出",
          dA == pre and recA == [])
    pi = {0: 3, 1: 2, 2: 1, 3: 0}; sig = {"A": "B", "B": "A", "C": "C", "R": "R"}
    h0 = ("A", "R", "C", "A")
    _, r1 = run_rec(dict(Xb), h0)
    _, r2 = run_rec({(pi[i], c): v for (i, c), v in Xb.items()}, tuple(sig[x] for x in h0))
    check("T13b 形成记录随改名交换：记录条目经 π 改标签后逐条相同（一例）",
          sorted((t, (pi[i], c), p_) for t, (i, c), p_ in r1) == sorted(r2))
    # ---------------- T14 实际方程、共同定义域、残留投影与三值合取 ----------------
    print("T14　实际方程、共同定义域、残留投影与 D18 三值合取（定理 18-4（二）（四））")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    # 实际方程 post(d + Σ原) 与 d + ΣF 的比较；共同定义域
    cap_post = lambda x: min(x, F(1))
    cap = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p", F(1))]},
                 lambda dn, do: {"x": cap_post(dn["x"])})
    _, after_raw, _, _ = C.step(cap, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
    f_after = F(1) + C.f_layer_update([(F(1), 1)], eps)
    check("T14a 初态 1、两边入账 +1（总量相等）：原机制 post(1 + 1) = %s，F 层 1 + 1 = %s；后态不同，入账总量相等不足以推出一拍相同" %
          (fs(after_raw["x"]), fs(f_after)),
          C.raw_update([(F(1), 1)]) == C.f_layer_update([(F(1), 1)], eps) and after_raw["x"] == 1 and f_after == 2
          and not C.f_post_match(cap_post, F(1), F(1), F(1)))
    ok_eq, ok_dom, n_case = True, True, 0
    for d0 in [F(0), F(1, 4), F(3, 4), F(1)]:
        for cs in ([(F(1, 5), 1)], [(F(1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 20), 1), (F(-1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 2), 1), (F(1, 20), 1)],
                   [(F(1, 5), 0)], [(F(1, 2), 1), (F(1, 2), 1)]):
            n_case += 1
            raw_sum = C.raw_update(cs); f_sum = C.f_layer_update(cs, eps)
            h = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda cs_: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p%d" % k, c) for k, (c, _) in enumerate(cs_)]}))(cs),
                       lambda dn, do: {"x": cap_post(dn["x"])})
            _, dr, _, _ = C.step(h, {"x": d0}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
            same = dr["x"] == d0 + f_sum
            if same != C.f_post_match(cap_post, d0, raw_sum, f_sum):
                ok_eq = False
            if cap_post(d0 + raw_sum) == d0 + raw_sum and cap_post(d0 + f_sum) == d0 + f_sum:
                if same != (raw_sum == f_sum):
                    ok_dom = False
    check("T14b 实际方程判据 post(d + Σ原) = d + ΣF 与一拍相同等价（%d 例，上限 1 的夹限）" % n_case, ok_eq)
    check("T14c 共同定义域（两边 post 在该拍都不触发）上，一拍相同 ⟺ 入账总量相等（同 %d 例中落在该域者）" % n_case, ok_dom)
    rd = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {"y": r}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("W_r", y["y"])]}, None, lambda r, y: r)
    tr0 = [dd_["x"] for dd_ in [C.run(rd, {"x": F(0)}, F(0), ("s",))[1]]]
    tr1 = [dd_["x"] for dd_ in [C.run(rd, {"x": F(0)}, F(1), ("s",))[1]]]
    check("T14d 有残留时：两完整态 (d, r) = (0, 0) 与 (0, 1) 的 d 投影相同，写入读残留，一拍后 d 为 %s 对 %s；F 只承载 d 时，轨迹相等只比较投影，须申报投影或忠实增广" %
          (fs(tr0[0]), fs(tr1[0])), tr0[0] != tr1[0])
    # D18 完整合取；0 优先于 ⊥
    law = ("lin", F(1), F(1), ("x",))
    vals3 = [0, 1, "⊥"]
    ok_tt = True
    for av in (0, 1):
        sx = {"x": F(1) if av else F(0)}
        for cr in vals3:
            for pa in vals3:
                got = C.histcond_F_full(law, sx, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, (lambda p_: (lambda _S: p_))(pa), cert_read=cr)
                if got != C.kleene_and([av, cr, pa if av else 0]):
                    ok_tt = False
    check("T14e HistCond_F 为三值合取 [Avail≠∅] ∧ cert_read ∧ Path：18 种组合逐一与「任一项 0 得 0，无 0 而有 ⊥ 得 ⊥」一致；读取认证 ⊥、路径 0 时为 0",
          ok_tt and C.histcond_F_full(law, {"x": F(1)}, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, lambda _: 0, cert_read="⊥") == 0)
    lawA = ("lin", F(1), F(1, 20), ("d",))
    atomsA, colA = C.readback_atoms(lawA, {"d": F(1)}, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, {"d": [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]}, lambda _: 1)
    check("T14f 三栏是摘要、原子保留：T10a 之例原子 Avail = %s、cert_read = %s、Path = %s、HistCond_F = %s、Act_F = %s、数值差「%s」，摘要「%s」；差异未获证不覆盖 HistCond_F = 1" %
          (atomsA["Avail"], atomsA["cert_read"], atomsA["Path"], atomsA["HistCond_F"], atomsA["Act_F"], atomsA["数值差"], colA),
          atomsA["HistCond_F"] == 1 and atomsA["数值差"] == "数值差非零未越阈" and colA == "函数支持已立")
    lawB = ("bil", F(0), F(1), "j1", "j2")
    sB = {"j1": F(1), "j2": F(0)}
    s2B = dict(sB); s2B["j2"] = F(1)
    big = abs(C.f_component(lawB, s2B) - C.f_component(lawB, sB))
    atomsB, colB = C.readback_atoms(lawB, sB, ["j1", "j2"], eps, {"j1", "j2"}, {"j1": [F(0)], "j2": [F(1)]}, lambda _: 1)
    check("T14g 有限差非零不等于实际态非零函数支持：c = s(j1)s(j2)，s = (1, 0)，改深度为 0 的 j2 为 1 得差 %s > ε（该对照不合定义 6′（乙）款），j1 的伙伴为 0：HistCond_F = %s，数值差「%s」，摘要「%s」" %
          (fs(big), atomsB["HistCond_F"], atomsB["数值差"], colB),
          big > eps and atomsB["HistCond_F"] == 0 and atomsB["数值差"] == "数值差为零" and colB == "有效差异未获证")
    print("T15　首次分项差、实际后态差与覆盖（定理 18-8（二）（四））")
    flyc = M.make_flyc()
    a0 = M.flyc_zero(); a0[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 10)
    a1 = dict(a0); a1[("c", 0)] = F(1)
    ya0, da0, _, ra0 = C.step(flyc, a0, None, "A")
    ya1, da1, _, ra1 = C.step(flyc, a1, None, "A")
    pre0 = a0[(0, "a3")] + sum(c for _, c in ra0["comps"][(0, "a3")])
    pre1 = a1[(0, "a3")] + sum(c for _, c in ra1["comps"][(0, "a3")])
    check("T15a FLYC：γ1 为 0、α3 深度 1/10、c 为 0 与 1，呈现 A：首次分项差在此拍出现（弛豫 %s 对 %s），夹限前 %s 对 %s，夹限后皆为 0：首次分项差不是首次实际后态差" %
          (fs([c for p_, c in ra0["comps"][(0, "a3")] if p_ == "RELAX_a3"][0]), fs([c for p_, c in ra1["comps"][(0, "a3")] if p_ == "RELAX_a3"][0]),
           fs(pre0), fs(pre1)),
          ra0["comps"][(0, "a3")] != ra1["comps"][(0, "a3")] and pre0 == F(-11, 80) and pre1 == F(-39, 400)
          and da0[(0, "a3")] == da1[(0, "a3")] == 0 and all(da0[p_] == da1[p_] for p_ in M.FLY_POS))
    _, dbk0, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a0, None, "R", eps, "booked")
    _, dbk1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a1, None, "R", eps, "booked")
    _, drw0, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a0, None, "R", eps, "raw")
    _, drw1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a1, None, "R", eps, "raw")
    check("T15b 有效入账可丢弃分项差：深度 1/10 静置，弛豫 −1/20 与 −1/100 都 ≤ ε = 1/10，入账语义下两臂后态同为 %s；原机制下为 %s 对 %s" %
          (fs(dbk0[(0, "a3")]), fs(drw0[(0, "a3")]), fs(drw1[(0, "a3")])),
          dbk0[(0, "a3")] == dbk1[(0, "a3")] == F(1, 10) and drw0[(0, "a3")] != drw1[(0, "a3")])
    b0 = M.flyc_zero(); b0[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    b1 = dict(b0); b1[("c", 0)] = F(1)
    _, db0, _, _ = C.step(flyc, b0, None, "R")
    _, db1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, b1, None, "R")
    check("T15c 共同未截断、原机制入账时实际后态差等于分项差 (ρ(c′) − ρ(c))·d_x：%s − %s = %s" %
          (fs(db1[(0, "a3")]), fs(db0[(0, "a3")]), fs(db1[(0, "a3")] - db0[(0, "a3")])),
          db1[(0, "a3")] - db0[(0, "a3")] == (M.RHO[1] - M.RHO[0]) * b0[(0, "a3")])
    # 覆盖须完整数学态相同；未覆盖的隐藏差可晚显；强训练不抹掉已产生的差
    H1 = M.flyc_zero(); H2 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        H1[(i, "g1")] = H2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); H2[("c", i)] = F(1)
    _, h1n, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H1, None, "A")
    _, h2n, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H2, None, "A")
    check("T15d 覆盖：强写入拍后两运行完整数学态（各位 d、c；无残留、无钟控计时、无存在变量）逐位相同，故此后逐拍相同", h1n == h2n)
    late = ("R",) * 6 + ("A", "R", "A")
    yl1, dl1, _ = C.run(flyc, H1, None, late)
    yl2, dl2, _ = C.run(flyc, H2, None, late)
    first = next((k for k in range(len(late)) if yl1[k] != yl2[k]), None)
    ppl = yl1[6]["PPL1_a3"]
    check("T15e 无覆盖时「此前无可读差」不推出永隐：同一初态对静置 6 拍后 PPL1-α3(A) = %s（> 0 而 ≤ θ_c，弱写入），第 %d 拍读数始分（MBON-α3(A) %s 对 %s）；长度 ≤ 4 的 T8g 不覆盖此史" %
          (fs(ppl), (first or 0) + 1, fs(yl1[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(yl2[-1]["MBON_a3"])),
          0 < ppl <= M.THETA_C and first == len(late) - 1 and all(yl1[k] == yl2[k] for k in range(len(late) - 1)))
    E1 = M.flyc_zero(); E2 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        E1[(i, "g1")] = E2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); E1[(i, "a3")] = E2[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2); E2[("c", i)] = F(1)
    ye1, de1, _ = C.run(flyc, E1, None, ("R", "A", "A"))
    ye2, de2, _ = C.run(flyc, E2, None, ("R", "A", "A"))
    check("T15f 强训练不抹掉已产生的状态差：深度 1/2 静置一拍已生差，其后强写入两臂 c 都置 1，α3 深度仍为 %s 对 %s，读数仍分" %
          (fs(de1[(0, "a3")]), fs(de2[(0, "a3")])),
          de1[("c", 0)] == de2[("c", 0)] == 1 and de1[(0, "a3")] != de2[(0, "a3")] and ye1[-1] != ye2[-1])
    print("T16　边界条件与可解性（定理 18-9（二）（乙）（丙））")
    n2, e2, D2 = [0, 1], [(0, 1), (0, 1)], [F(1), F(1)]
    q_old = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, D2, {0: 1, 1: 0}, {})
    q_new = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, [F(0), F(1)], {0: 1, 1: 0}, {})
    T_inj = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, [F(0), F(1)], {1: 0}, {0: 1})
    T0 = M.removal_response(n2, e2, D2, {0: 1, 1: 0}, 0)
    check("T16a 两端定势 1 与 0、并联两支导率各 1：移除一支，另一支流量 %s → %s；套用定注入传递 T = %s 预言改变 +%s（不符）；同一定势边界下的增量响应 T⁰ = %s，预言 0（相符）" %
          (fs(q_old[1]), fs(q_new[1]), fs(T_inj[1]), fs(q_old[0] * T_inj[1]), fs(T0[1])),
          q_new[1] - q_old[1] == 0 and q_old[0] * T_inj[1] == 1 and q_old[0] * T0[1] == q_new[1] - q_old[1])
    qi_old = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, D2, {1: 0}, {0: 2})
    qi_new = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, [F(0), F(1)], {1: 0}, {0: 2})
    check("T16b 同一网络改为固定注入 2：移除一支，另一支 %s → %s，ΔQ = Q_e·T(u→v) = %s" % (fs(qi_old[1]), fs(qi_new[1]), fs(qi_old[0] * T_inj[1])),
          qi_new[1] - qi_old[1] == qi_old[0] * T_inj[1])
    n4, e4 = [0, 1, 2, 3], [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D4 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    ok_bc, n_bc = True, 0
    for fixed, inj in (({0: 1, 3: 0}, {}), ({0: 1, 3: 0}, {2: F(1, 2)}), ({3: 0}, {0: F(1)}), ({3: 0}, {0: F(1), 2: F(-1, 3)})):
        q0 = M.flows_bc(n4, e4, D4, fixed, inj)
        for k in range(5):
            Dk = list(D4); Dk[k] = F(0)
            qk = M.flows_bc(n4, e4, Dk, fixed, inj)
            Tk = M.removal_response(n4, e4, D4, fixed, k)
            n_bc += 1
            if any(qk[x] - q0[x] != q0[k] * Tk[x] for x in range(5) if x != k):
                ok_bc = False
    check("T16c 增量响应式 ΔQ_x = Q_e·T⁰_x(u→v)（T⁰：同一边界、定势端增量为 0）在惠斯通网上逐管成立：定势、混合、定注入四种边界 × 五条移除边 = %d 例" % n_bc, ok_bc)
    n5, e5, D5 = [0, 1, 2, 4], [(0, 1), (0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 4)], [F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1)]
    q5 = M.flows_bc(n5, e5, D5, {1: 0}, {0: 1})
    D5c = list(D5); D5c[3] = F(0)
    q5c = M.flows_bc(n5, e5, D5c, {1: 0}, {0: 1})
    try:
        M.removal_response(n5, e5, D5, {1: 0}, 3); solv = True
    except ValueError:
        solv = False
    check("T16d 桥断开（悬挂支）：Q_e = %s，移除后各流量不变；去掉后 u、v 不连通，单位注入问题无解，不调用移除式" % fs(q5[3]),
          q5[3] == 0 and q5c[:3] == q5[:3] and not solv)
    n6, e6, D6 = [0, 1, 3], [(0, 1), (1, 3)], [F(1), F(1)]
    try:
        M.flows_bc(n6, e6, [F(0), F(1)], {3: 0}, {0: 1}); cons = True
    except ValueError:
        cons = False
    check("T16e 桥断开（源汇之间）：去掉 (0,1) 后源所在分量 {0} 注入 1 而无定势结点，分量守恒不成立，稳态无解，不调用移除式", not cons)
    # ---------------- T17 数值差与定义 6′ 完整证书 ----------------
    print("T17　数值差与〔十七〕定义 6′ 完整证书（定理 18-4 引言）")
    lawU = ("lin", F(1), F(1), ("x",))
    sU = {"x": F(1)}
    cU = {"x": [F(0)]}
    def rb(path_value, eff, ev=None):
        return C.readback_atoms(lawU, sU, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: path_value, cert_eff=eff, evidence=ev)
    a1, c1 = rb(1, 0)
    check("T17a（无作用）c = 1 + x，x = 1 → 0，数值差 1：Path = 1、cert_eff = 0：数值差「%s」，Act_F = %s，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」，不给「有效差异已获证」" %
          (a1["数值差"], a1["Act_F"], a1["完整证书"], c1),
          a1["数值差"] == "数值差越阈" and a1["Act_F"] == 0 and a1["完整证书"] == 0 and c1 == "函数支持已立" and a1["HistCond_F"] == 1)
    a2, c2 = rb(0, 1)
    check("T17b（缺途径）Path = 0、cert_eff = 1：途径记 %s（路径 0 不排除另外的物质途径，亦不由数值差反推），完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」；数值差「%s」保留" %
          (a2["定义6′四款"]["途径"], a2["完整证书"], c2, a2["数值差"]),
          a2["定义6′四款"]["途径"] == "⊥" and a2["完整证书"] == "⊥" and c2 == "有效差异未获证" and a2["数值差"] == "数值差越阈" and a2["HistCond_F"] == 0)
    a3, c3 = rb("⊥", "⊥")
    check("T17c（认证未知）Path = ⊥、cert_eff = ⊥：Act_F = %s，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」；数值证据与未知保留" % (a3["Act_F"], a3["完整证书"], c3),
          a3["Act_F"] == "⊥" and a3["完整证书"] == "⊥" and c3 == "有效差异未获证" and a3["HistCond_F"] == "⊥" and a3["数值差"] == "数值差越阈")
    a4, c4 = rb(1, 1)
    check("T17d（所有前件成立）Path = 1、cert_eff = 1，同拍与承担位由构造给出：四款 %s，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」" %
          ("、".join("%s %s" % kv for kv in a4["定义6′四款"].items()), a4["完整证书"], c4),
          a4["完整证书"] == 1 and c4 == "有效差异已获证")
    a5, c5 = rb(0, 1, {"途径": 1})
    a6, c6 = rb(1, 1, {"同拍": "⊥"})
    a7, c7 = rb(1, 1, {"承担位": 0})
    check("T17e 显式证据接口：Path = 0 而另申报合法途径并独立带证书（途径 = 1）时完整证书 %s、摘要「%s」，HistCond_F 仍为 %s（〔封二〕原式不改）；同拍认证未知时 %s；承担位不成立时 %s" %
          (a5["完整证书"], c5, a5["HistCond_F"], c6, c7),
          a5["完整证书"] == 1 and c5 == "有效差异已获证" and a5["HistCond_F"] == 0 and c6 == "函数支持已立" and a6["完整证书"] == "⊥"
          and c7 == "函数支持已立" and a7["完整证书"] == 0)
    tt_ok = True
    for av in (0, 1):
        for cr in (0, 1, "⊥"):
            for pa in (0, 1, "⊥"):
                sx = {"x": F(1) if av else F(0)}
                pf = (lambda p_: (lambda _S: p_))(pa)
                if C.histcond_F_full(lawU, sx, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, pf, cr) != C.kleene_and([av, cr, pa if av else 0]):
                    tt_ok = False
    check("T17f 〔封二〕D18 原合取与按原条件回读保留：histcond_F_full 在 18 种组合上与三值合取同值；readback_hist 返回两列原始判断（函数支持已立 / 数值差越阈），不给完整证书",
          tt_ok and C.readback_hist(lawU, sU, ["x"], eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: 1) == ("函数支持已立", "数值差越阈"))
    # ---------------- T18 作用证据的合并 ----------------
    print("T18　作用证据的合并：否证、未证、冲突与独立见证（定理 18-4 引言）")
    v1, cv1 = rb(1, 0, {"作用": 1})
    check("T18a（外加作用声称不覆盖否证）c = 1 + x，x 由 1 设 0，Path = 1、cert_eff = 0，外加 evidence = {作用: 1}：Act_F = %s，作用款 %s，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」，冲突记录「%s」" %
          (v1["Act_F"], v1["定义6′四款"]["作用"], v1["完整证书"], cv1, "；".join(v1["证据冲突"])),
          v1["Act_F"] == 0 and v1["定义6′四款"]["作用"] == 0 and v1["完整证书"] == 0 and cv1 != "有效差异已获证"
          and len(v1["证据冲突"]) == 1)
    v2, cv2 = rb(1, 0)
    check("T18b（否证）同例不加外部声称：作用来源「%s」，完整证书 %s，无冲突记录，摘要「%s」" % (v2["作用来源"], v2["完整证书"], cv2),
          v2["作用来源"] == "F 原子否证" and v2["完整证书"] == 0 and v2["证据冲突"] == [] and cv2 == "函数支持已立")
    v3, cv3 = rb(1, "⊥")
    check("T18c（未证）cert_eff = ⊥、无外部认证：作用款 %s，来源「%s」，完整证书 %s，无冲突，摘要「%s」" % (v3["定义6′四款"]["作用"], v3["作用来源"], v3["完整证书"], cv3),
          v3["定义6′四款"]["作用"] == "⊥" and v3["作用来源"] == "未证" and v3["完整证书"] == "⊥" and v3["证据冲突"] == [] and cv3 == "函数支持已立")
    v4, cv4 = rb(1, "⊥", {"作用": 1})
    check("T18d（未证由显式认证补足）cert_eff = ⊥、evidence = {作用: 1}：来源「%s」，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」；Act_F 原子仍为 %s" % (v4["作用来源"], v4["完整证书"], cv4, v4["Act_F"]),
          v4["作用来源"] == "显式认证" and v4["完整证书"] == 1 and cv4 == "有效差异已获证" and v4["Act_F"] == "⊥")
    v5, cv5 = rb(1, 1, {"作用": 0})
    check("T18e（冲突的另一向）Act_F = 1 而外部声称 0：记冲突，作用款 0，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」" % (v5["完整证书"], cv5),
          len(v5["证据冲突"]) == 1 and v5["完整证书"] == 0 and cv5 == "函数支持已立")
    v6, cv6 = rb(1, 1)
    v6b, cv6b = rb(1, 1, {"作用": 1})
    check("T18f（完整正例）Path = 1、cert_eff = 1：完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」；外部声称一致（作用 1）时同，无冲突" % (v6["完整证书"], cv6),
          v6["完整证书"] == 1 and cv6 == "有效差异已获证" and v6b["完整证书"] == 1 and v6b["证据冲突"] == [] and cv6b == cv6)
    v7, cv7 = rb(0, 1, {"途径": 1})
    check("T18g（显式途径认证补足 F 路径未获证，原设计保留）Path = 0、evidence = {途径: 1}：完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」，HistCond_F = %s" % (v7["完整证书"], cv7, v7["HistCond_F"]),
          v7["完整证书"] == 1 and cv7 == "有效差异已获证" and v7["HistCond_F"] == 0 and v7["证据冲突"] == [])
    v9, cv9 = rb(1, "⊥", {"作用": "⊥"})
    check("T18h（显式未证）cert_eff = ⊥、evidence = {作用: ⊥}：来源「%s」（不标「显式认证」），作用款 %s，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」，与 T18c 的三值判定和摘要相同" %
          (v9["作用来源"], v9["定义6′四款"]["作用"], v9["完整证书"], cv9),
          v9["作用来源"] == "显式未证" and v9["定义6′四款"]["作用"] == "⊥" and v9["完整证书"] == v3["完整证书"] == "⊥" and cv9 == cv3 and v9["证据冲突"] == [])
    indep = {"事件身份": "另一机制 ρ′ 的同拍事件", "模式": "R 层", "认证依据": "申报的独立途径与作用证据", "作用": 1}
    v8, cv8 = C.readback_atoms(lawU, sU, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: 1, cert_eff=0, independent=indep)
    v8b, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawU, sU, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: 1, cert_eff=0, independent={"作用": 1})
    check("T18i（独立见证单列）cert_eff = 0 时附不同机制的 R 层见证：见证按事件身份、模式与认证依据单列，本 F 事件完整证书仍为 %s、摘要「%s」；字段不全时记「%s」" %
          (v8["完整证书"], cv8, v8b["独立见证"]),
          v8["独立见证"] == indep and v8["完整证书"] == 0 and cv8 == "函数支持已立" and v8b["独立见证"] == "独立见证不完整")
    # ---------------- T19 果蝇代表模型的首达区间（第十一节） ----------------
    print("T19　果蝇代表模型：再暴露翻转延迟的首达区间（第十一节 PN-1）")
    def nstar(D):
        n_ = 0
        while -M.A_INN + 2 * M.B_DIS * D * M.R1 ** n_ > 0:
            n_ += 1
        return n_
    def interval(n_):
        lo = M.A_INN / (2 * M.B_DIS * M.R1 ** (n_ - 1)) if n_ >= 1 else F(0)
        return lo, M.A_INN / (2 * M.B_DIS * M.R1 ** n_)
    check("T19a 同一平台：D = 1/2 与 51/100 同在第 3 拍翻转", nstar(F(1, 2)) == nstar(F(51, 100)) == 3)
    check("T19b 区间式 a/(2b r1^(n−1)) < D ≤ a/(2b r1^n) 与 n*(D) 在 D = 0.40..1.00（步长 1/200）上逐点一致",
          all(interval(nstar(D))[0] < D <= interval(nstar(D))[1] for D in [F(k, 200) for k in range(80, 201)]))
    (l3, h3), (l9, h9) = interval(3), interval(9)
    m3, m9 = (l3 + h3) / 2, (l9 + h9) / 2
    dlt = min((h3 - l3) / 2, (h9 - l9) / 2) * F(9, 10)
    check("T19c n = 3 与 n = 9 区间中点 %s、%s，误差 δ = %s 内各自保持首达拍 3 与 9（端点精确核验）" % (fs(m3), fs(m9), fs(dlt)),
          nstar(m3 - dlt) == nstar(m3 + dlt) == 3 and nstar(m9 - dlt) == nstar(m9 + dlt) == 9)
    y_f = M.fly_read(M.fly_zero(), None, "A")
    probe_ok = True
    for k in range(0, 13):
        dd_ = M.fly_zero()
        for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
            dd_[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4) * M.R1 ** k
        dd_[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
        y_ = M.fly_read(dd_, None, "A")
        c_ = [c for p_, c in M.fly_comps(dd_, y_, "A")[(0, "a3")] if p_ == "W_a3"][0]
        if (c_ > 0) != (-M.A_INN + 2 * M.B_DIS * F(3, 4) * M.R1 ** k > 0):
            probe_ok = False
    check("T19d 再暴露的 α3 写入符号由 γ1 深度经 PPL1-α3 决定（势在写入位处的值）：k = 0..12 拍逐拍与 −a + 2bD·r1^k 同号", probe_ok)
    # ---------------- T20 有效入账下的改名等变（定理 18-2（一）） ----------------
    print("T20　有效入账下的改名等变：筛后写入交换须另证（定理 18-2（一））")
    eb = F(1, 10)
    sw = {"x": "z", "z": "x"}
    hs = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: dict(d),
                lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("A", F(3, 20)), ("B", F(1, 20))], "z": [("A", F(1, 5)), ("B", F(0))]})
    d00 = {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}
    _, raw00, _, _ = C.step(hs, d00, None, "R", eb, "raw")
    _, bk00, _, _ = C.step(hs, d00, None, "R", eb, "booked")
    cs00 = hs.comps(d00, hs.read(d00, None, "R"), "R")
    check("T20a 总写入与改名交换（两位总写入同为 %s）而筛后写入不交换：对称初态 (0, 0) 的原机制后态 (%s, %s) 被交换固定，有效入账后态 (%s, %s) 不被固定；过程配对条件不成立" %
          (fs(raw00["x"]), fs(raw00["x"]), fs(raw00["z"]), fs(bk00["x"]), fs(bk00["z"])),
          raw00["x"] == raw00["z"] and bk00["x"] != bk00["z"] and not C.pairing_ok(cs00, cs00, sw)
          and sum(c for _, c in cs00["x"]) == sum(c for _, c in cs00["z"]))
    hs2 = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: dict(d),
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("A", F(3, 20)), ("B", F(1, 20))], "z": [("A", F(3, 20)), ("B", F(1, 20))]})
    cs2 = hs2.comps(d00, hs2.read(d00, None, "R"), "R")
    _, bk2, _, _ = C.step(hs2, d00, None, "R", eb, "booked")
    check("T20b 过程配对逐分项保数值时（两位分项同为 (3/20, 1/20)），有效入账后态 (%s, %s) 被交换固定" % (fs(bk2["x"]), fs(bk2["z"])),
          C.pairing_ok(cs2, cs2, sw) and bk2["x"] == bk2["z"])
    ok_pair, ok_eq = True, True
    for pi, sig, tau in G4:
        pmap = {(i, c): (pi[i], tau[c]) for (i, c) in M.FLY_POS}
        for L_ in range(3):
            for h in product("ABCR", repeat=L_):
                d1 = dict(X0); d2 = g_apply(pi, tau, X0)
                for x_ in h:
                    y1 = fly.read(d1, None, x_); y2 = fly.read(d2, None, sig[x_])
                    if not C.pairing_ok(fly.comps(d1, y1, x_), fly.comps(d2, y2, sig[x_]), pmap):
                        ok_pair = False
                    _, d1, _, _ = C.step(fly, d1, None, x_, eb, "booked")
                    _, d2, _, _ = C.step(fly, d2, None, sig[x_], eb, "booked")
                    if g_apply(pi, tau, d1) != d2:
                        ok_eq = False
    check("T20c FLY 模型：G4 逐元素满足过程配对（逐分项保数值），有效入账（ε = 1/10）下在长度 ≤ 2 的全部史上等变", ok_pair and ok_eq)
    Uu = M.fly_zero(); Uu[(0, "g1")] = F(1)
    Vv = M.fly_zero(); Vv[(0, "g1")] = Vv[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    sb, nb_ = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (Uu, None), (Vv, None), "ABR", 5, eb, "booked")
    sr, nr_ = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (Uu, None), (Vv, None), "ABR", 5, F(0), "raw")
    check("T20d U、V 的精确动力同一分语义复跑（长度 ≤ 5 的 %d 条 ABR 史）：原机制 %s，有效入账（ε = 1/10）%s" %
          (nb_, "成立" if sr else "不成立", "成立" if sb else "不成立"), sr and sb and nb_ == nr_)
    hce = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p", F(1))]})
    _, dnum, _, _ = C.step(hce, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eb, "booked")
    cs_ce = hce.comps({"x": F(1)}, {}, "s")
    wF = F(1) + C.booked_sum_F(cs_ce["x"], eb, {"p": 0})
    check("T20e 两种筛后算子：同一分项 +1（> ε）而 cert_eff = 0，纯数值筛后 W^ε_num 后态 %s，F 层认证筛后 W^ε_F 后态 %s" % (fs(dnum["x"]), fs(wF)),
          dnum["x"] == 2 and wF == 1)
    hpc = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: dict(d), lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("A", F(1, 5))], "z": [("A", F(1, 5))]})
    cpc = hpc.comps(d00, d00, "R")
    certA = {("A", "x"): 1, ("A", "z"): 0}
    numx, numz = C.booked_sum(cpc["x"], eb), C.booked_sum(cpc["z"], eb)
    Fx = C.booked_sum_F(cpc["x"], eb, {"A": certA[("A", "x")]}); Fz = C.booked_sum_F(cpc["z"], eb, {"A": certA[("A", "z")]})
    check("T20f 配对保数值与阈值而不保认证：交换 x、z 下 W^ε_num 交换（%s、%s），W^ε_F 不交换（%s、%s）；cert_pairing_ok 判不成立" %
          (fs(numx), fs(numz), fs(Fx), fs(Fz)),
          C.pairing_ok(cpc, cpc, sw) and numx == numz and Fx != Fz and not C.cert_pairing_ok(cpc, certA, certA, sw))
    # ---------------- T21 后处理与局部性、无创、隐变判据（定理 18-3、18-7） ----------------
    print("T21　后处理与局部性、无创、隐变判据（定理 18-3、18-7）")
    hp = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {}, lambda dn, do: {"x": dn["x"] + dn["z"], "z": dn["z"]})
    vx = [C.step(hp, {"x": F(0), "z": zz}, None, "R")[1]["x"] for zz in (F(0), F(1))]
    check("T21a 跨位后处理 post(d)_x = d_x + d_z：LW 成立（写入恒等、x 的局部活动恒定），只改 z 而 Δd_x 由 %s 变为 %s；定理 18-3 的后态结论须逐位后处理前件" % (fs(vx[0]), fs(vx[1])),
          vx[0] != vx[1])
    hpw = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("w", F(1, 4))]},
                 lambda dn, do: {"x": min(dn["x"], F(1)), "z": dn["z"]})
    _, dpw, _, rpw = C.step(hpw, {"x": F(1), "z": F(0)}, None, "s")
    check("T21b 饱和夹限下的无创：门开、分项 1/4 非零（Act⁰ = {x}），实际后态 d′ = d：无创读势以实际后态定义",
          rpw["Act0"] == {"x"} and dpw == {"x": F(1), "z": F(0)})
    hsw = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"read_x": d["x"]}, lambda d, y, s: {}, lambda dn, do: {"x": dn["z"], "z": dn["x"]})
    arms = [{"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}, {"x": F(0), "z": F(1)}]
    ysw = [C.run(hsw, a_, None, ("R", "R"))[0] for a_ in arms]
    Bsw = [[F(0), F(1)], [F(1), F(0)]]
    naive = C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1), F(0)], [F(0), F(1)]], [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)])
    withB = C.hidden_forever_lti(Bsw, [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)])
    check("T21c 仿射后处理（交换两位）、只读 x：两态首拍同读、次拍可分（%s 对 %s）；略去后处理（A_j = I）的判据误判永隐，并入其线性部分 B_j 后判显现" %
          (fs(ysw[0][1]["read_x"]), fs(ysw[1][1]["read_x"])),
          ysw[0][0] == ysw[1][0] and ysw[0][1] != ysw[1][1] and naive and not withB)
    hhalf = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"read_x": d["x"]}, lambda d, y, s: {}, lambda dn, do: {"x": dn["x"] / 2, "z": dn["z"] / 2})
    same_h, _ = C.exact_dyn_same(hhalf, (arms[0], None), (arms[1], None), "R", 8)
    check("T21d 逐位仿射后处理（各位乘 1/2）、只读 x：并入 B_j = diag(1/2, 1/2) 的判据判永隐，与 8 拍实跑一致",
          same_h and C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1, 2), F(0)], [F(0), F(1, 2)]], [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)]))
    Ua2 = M.fly_zero(); Va2 = M.fly_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Ua2[(i, "g1")] = Va2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    Ua2[(0, "a3")] = F(1); Va2[(0, "a3")] = Va2[(1, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    yu = C.run(fly, Ua2, None, ("A", "A"))[0]; yv = C.run(fly, Va2, None, ("A", "A"))[0]
    check("T21e 饱和（夹限）不是共同仿射后处理，不入定理 18-7（一）：Ua、Va 首次同读、第二次可分（同 T2g）", yu[0] == yv[0] and yu[1] != yv[1])
    hsq = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"y": d["x"] + d["z"]},
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("square", d["x"] * d["x"]), ("rest", 1 - d["x"] * d["x"])]},
                 lambda dn, do: {k_: v_ / 8 for k_, v_ in dn.items()})
    st2 = ({"x": F(0), "z": F(1, 4)}, {"x": F(1, 4), "z": F(0)})
    pb = [C.step(hsq, d_, None, "R", eb, "booked") for d_ in st2]
    pr = [C.step(hsq, d_, None, "R", eb, "raw") for d_ in st2]
    nb2 = [hsq.read(q_[1], None, "R")["y"] for q_ in pb]
    nr2 = [hsq.read(q_[1], None, "R")["y"] for q_ in pr]
    crit = C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1, 8), F(0)], [F(0), F(1, 8)]], [[F(1), F(1)]], [F(1, 4), F(-1, 4)])
    check("T21f 同一筛选集合而筛后写入非仿射：x 的分项 x²、1 − x²，ε = 1/10，域 [0, 1/4] 内两臂只入账后一项；原机制斜率给 A = I、B = I/8，判据判永隐（%s）；原机制下次拍读数 %s、%s 相同，有效入账下为 %s、%s 可分" %
          (crit, fs(nr2[0]), fs(nr2[1]), fs(nb2[0]), fs(nb2[1])),
          pb[0][0] == pb[1][0] and pb[0][3]["Acte"] == pb[1][3]["Acte"] == {"x"} and crit and nr2[0] == nr2[1]
          and nb2 == [F(5, 32), F(19, 128)])
    # ---------------- T22 对照身份与证书绑定（定理 18-4 引言） ----------------
    print("T22　对照身份与证书绑定：申报对照族逐条计算（定理 18-4 引言）")
    lawJ = ("lin", F(1), F(3, 50), ("x", "z"))
    sJ = {"x": F(1), "z": F(1)}
    a1_, c1_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, {"x": [F(0)], "z": [F(0)]}, lambda _: 1)
    check("T22a 仅单设（单设简写不自动生成联合对照）：两条单设差各 3/50，数值差「%s」，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」" % (a1_["数值差"], a1_["完整证书"], c1_),
          a1_["数值差"] == "数值差非零未越阈" and a1_["完整证书"] == 0 and c1_ == "函数支持已立")
    famJ = {"对照": [{"id": "x 单设", "set": {"x": F(0)}}, {"id": "z 单设", "set": {"z": F(0)}},
                     {"id": "x、z 联合", "set": {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    a2_, c2_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famJ, lambda _: 1)
    check("T22b 合法联合（联合对照作为一条显式申报）：联合差 3/25 > ε，取证对照「%s」，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」" % (a2_["取证对照"], a2_["完整证书"], c2_),
          a2_["完整证书"] == 1 and a2_["取证对照"] == "x、z 联合" and c2_ == "有效差异已获证")
    famM = {"对照": [{"id": "x 单设", "set": {"x": F(0)}, "证据": {"途径": 1}},
                     {"id": "x、z 联合", "set": {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}, "证据": {"途径": "⊥"}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    a3_, c3_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famM, lambda _: 0)
    check("T22c 差与证书错配：越阈差属联合对照而其途径未证，途径证书属单设对照而其差 3/50 未越阈；逐条结果 %s，不拼接，完整证书 %s" %
          ([(k, fs(v), cc) for k, v, cc, _ in a3_["对照明细"]], a3_["完整证书"]),
          a3_["完整证书"] == "⊥" and a3_["取证对照"] is None and c3_ != "有效差异已获证")
    famS = {"对照": [{"id": "x、z 联合", "set": {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}, "证据": {"途径": 1}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    a4_, c4_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famS, lambda _: 0)
    check("T22d 同一对照完整成立：越阈差与途径证书属同一条联合对照，完整证书 %s，取证对照「%s」" % (a4_["完整证书"], a4_["取证对照"]),
          a4_["完整证书"] == 1 and a4_["取证对照"] == "x、z 联合")
    famI = {"对照": [{"id": "x 单设", "set": {"x": F(0)}}], "覆盖": "不完整"}
    a5_, c5_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famI, lambda _: 0)
    check("T22e 数值未越阈与对照域覆盖不足分开：申报覆盖不完整而所列对照无一越阈时记「%s」，摘要「%s」，不读作否定" % (a5_["数值差"], c5_),
          a5_["数值差"] == "对照域覆盖不足" and c5_ == "有效差异未获证")
    lawP = ("lin", F(1), F(1), ("x", "z"))
    sP = {"x": F(1), "z": F(1)}
    famZ = {"对照": [{"id": "z 单设", "set": {"z": F(0)}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    a6_, c6_ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famZ, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    check("T22f 聚合路径真、实际改动起点路径假：c = 1 + x + z，只有 x 有认证路径，冻结对照只令 z 归零；聚合 Path = %s（独立原子），z 单设差 %s，该条途径 %s，完整证书 %s，摘要「%s」" %
          (a6_["Path"], fs(a6_["对照明细"][0][1]), a6_["对照明细"][0][3]["途径"], a6_["完整证书"], c6_),
          a6_["Path"] == 1 and a6_["对照明细"][0][3]["途径"] == "⊥" and a6_["完整证书"] == "⊥" and c6_ == "函数支持已立")
    famZ2 = {"对照": [{"id": "z 单设", "set": {"z": F(0)}, "证据": {"途径": 1}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    a7_, c7_ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famZ2, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    check("T22g 同一对照另有已证实际途径：z 单设对照自带途径证书 1，完整证书 %s，取证对照「%s」，其四款 %s" %
          (a7_["完整证书"], a7_["取证对照"], a7_["定义6′四款"]),
          a7_["完整证书"] == 1 and a7_["取证对照"] == "z 单设" and a7_["定义6′四款"]["途径"] == 1)
    famI2 = {"对照": [{"id": "x 单设", "set": {"x": F(0)}}], "覆盖": "不完整"}
    a8_, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famI2, lambda _: 1)
    check("T22h 覆盖不完整时分列：证书状态「%s」" % a8_["证书状态"], a8_["证书状态"] == "已列对照证书析取为 0；申报域尚未覆盖")
    famE1 = {"对照": [{"id": "z 单设", "set": {"z": F(0)}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    famE2 = {"对照": [{"id": "z 单设（另写 x 原值）", "set": {"x": F(1), "z": F(0)}}], "覆盖": "完整"}
    e1, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famE1, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    e2, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famE2, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    check("T22i 同一对照状态的两种编码：{z: 0} 与 {x: 1, z: 0}（x 本为 1，未改）按实际差集归一后证书相同（%s、%s），未改的 x 不入起点" %
          (e1["完整证书"], e2["完整证书"]), e1["完整证书"] == e2["完整证书"] == "⊥"
          and e1["对照明细"][0][3] == e2["对照明细"][0][3])
    try:
        C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"},
                         {"对照": [{"id": "k", "set": {"z": F(0)}}, {"id": "k", "set": {"x": F(0)}}]}, lambda st: 1)
        dup = False
    except ValueError:
        dup = True
    check("T22j 对照身份须唯一：同名两条对照被拒绝；获证条目的四款直接保存，不经同名字典", dup)
    # ---------------- T23 J₁₈：经〔封二〕求值器的三条件（定理 18-4（四）） ----------------
    print("T23　J₁₈ 的三条件：经〔封二〕求值器 field_final 校验 D20、复现轨迹、求 D19 资格（定理 18-4（四））")
    w = (1, 2)
    def j18c(*a, **k):
        """T23a—j 的各例：证据协议申报证据图闭合。"""
        return C.j18_check(*a, closed_graph=True, **k)
    pulse_x = {"类": "脉冲", "名": "S0", "拍": 0, "目标": "x", "量": F(1, 2), "界外": True, "发生": 1}
    def proc(name, t, c_eff=1, c_read=1, k=F(1, 4), b=F(0), law="linear", reads=("x",), inside=True):
        return {"类": "过程", "名": name, "拍": t, "目标": "x", "载体在内": inside, "律": law, "读取": reads,
                "系数": k, "偏置": b, "cert_eff": c_eff, "cert_read": c_read}
    lk = [("state:x:1", "P1", 1)]
    jz = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, c_eff="⊥", k=F(0), b=F(0), law="constant", reads=())])
    check("T23a 零分项而作用认证未定：D20 %s；不运行 F，%s" % (jz["D20"], jz["R"]),
          jz["D20"].startswith("拒绝") and jz["事件表"] == 0 and jz["Q_F"] == "未运行")
    ju = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, c_eff="⊥")], lk)
    check("T23b 非零分项而作用认证未定：D20 %s；R 层待定另报" % ju["D20"], ju["D20"].startswith("拒绝") and ju["R"].startswith("R 层待定"))
    j0 = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, c_eff=0, k=F(0), b=F(1), law="constant", reads=())])
    check("T23c cert_eff = 0（已决定，D20 接受）：常数分项 +1，原机制轨迹 %s、F 轨迹 %s，轨迹条件 %s；F 原子 Act_F = %s" %
          ([fs(a["x"]) for a in j0["原机制轨迹"]], [fs(F(a["x"])) for a in j0["F 轨迹"]], j0["轨迹"], j0["事件"][0]["Act_F"]),
          j0["D20"] == "接受" and j0["轨迹"] == 0 and j0["事件"][0]["Act_F"] == 0)
    jt = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",),
                     [{"类": "过程", "名": "G0", "拍": 0, "目标": "x", "载体在内": True, "律": "constant", "读取": (), "偏置": F(1, 2)},
                      proc("P1", 1)], [("state:x:1", "P1", 1)])
    check("T23d 合格的作用而 Trace_F = 0：x 由场内续行形成而无界外成因（Mark 为空），P1 的 Act_F = %s、HistCond_F = %s，Trace_F = %s，Q_F = %s" %
          (jt["事件"][0]["Act_F"], jt["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"], jt["Trace_F"], jt["Q_F"]),
          jt["事件"][0]["Act_F"] == 1 and jt["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"] == 1 and jt["Trace_F"] == 0 and jt["Q_F"] == 0)
    jr = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("R1", 1, k=F(0), b=F(0))])
    check("T23e 纯读取：分项 0、读取已认证，Cond_F = %s、Act_F = %s，Part_F = %s；轨迹 %s" %
          (jr["事件"][0]["Cond_F"], jr["事件"][0]["Act_F"], jr["Part_F"], jr["轨迹"]),
          jr["事件"][0]["Cond_F"] == 1 and jr["事件"][0]["Act_F"] == 0 and jr["Part_F"] == 1 and jr["轨迹"] == 1)
    js = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",),
                     [dict(pulse_x, 拍=1), proc("P1", 1)], [("state:x:1", "P1", 1)])
    jp = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1)], lk)
    check("T23f Form 须早于当前拍且有来源：界外脉冲与读取同在拍 1 时 HistCond_F = %s；界外脉冲在拍 0（早于读取，来源为界外）时 HistCond_F = %s、Trace_F = %s" %
          (js["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"], jp["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"], jp["Trace_F"]),
          js["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"] == 0 and jp["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"] == 1 and jp["Trace_F"] == 1)
    check("T23g 同表正例：D20 接受，原机制轨迹 %s 与 F 轨迹逐拍相同，Trace_F = Part_F = HDyn_F = Q_F = 1" %
          [fs(a["x"]) for a in jp["原机制轨迹"]],
          jp["D20"] == "接受" and jp["轨迹"] == 1 and jp["Trace_F"] == jp["Part_F"] == jp["HDyn_F"] == jp["Q_F"] == 1)
    rb_ = C.readback_atoms(("lin", F(0), F(1, 4), ("x",)), {"x": F(1, 2)}, {"x"}, eb, {"x"}, {"x": [F(0)]}, lambda _: 1)[0]
    check("T23h 同一事件两侧原子对拍：本篇回读（拍 1 前态 x = 1/2）的 Act_F = %s、HistCond_F = %s 与 F 求值器事件 P1 的 Act_F = %s、HistCond_F = %s 相同" %
          (rb_["Act_F"], rb_["HistCond_F"], jp["事件"][0]["Act_F"], jp["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"]),
          (rb_["Act_F"], rb_["HistCond_F"]) == (jp["事件"][0]["Act_F"], jp["事件"][0]["HistCond_F"]) == (1, 1))
    jn = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, k=F(-3), b=F(0))], lk)
    check("T23i 负更新越出 D20：分项 −3/2 使更新值小于零，D20 %s" % jn["D20"], jn["D20"].startswith("拒绝"))
    enc, back = C.nonneg_encode({"x": F(-1, 2), "z": F(1, 4)})
    check("T23j 签名深度的非负编码：d = d⁺ − d⁻，各分量非负，回读还原 (−1/2, 1/4)",
          all(v >= 0 for v in enc.values()) and back(enc) == {"x": F(-1, 2), "z": F(1, 4)})
    jdec = C.j18_check(("x",), (0.2,), (0,), F(1, 100), ("x",), [{"类": "脉冲", "名": "u", "拍": 0, "目标": "x", "量": 0.1}], closed_graph=True)
    check("T23k 十进制脉冲：初值 0.2、同拍脉冲 0.1，两臂都按十进制精确解释，F 轨迹 %s、原机制轨迹 %s，轨迹条件 %s" %
          (fs(F(jdec["F 轨迹"][0]["x"])), fs(jdec["原机制轨迹"][0]["x"]), jdec["轨迹"]),
          jdec["D20"] == "接受" and jdec["轨迹"] == 1 and jdec["原机制轨迹"][0]["x"] == F(3, 10))
    jdc = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",),
                      [dict(pulse_x, 量=0.5), dict(proc("P1", 1), 系数=0.1, 偏置=0.3)], lk, closed_graph=True)
    check("T23l 十进制读写系数：脉冲 0.5、线性律系数 0.1、偏置 0.3，原机制轨迹 %s 与 F 轨迹逐拍相同" %
          [fs(a["x"]) for a in jdc["原机制轨迹"]], jdc["D20"] == "接受" and jdc["轨迹"] == 1)
    check("T23m 已有真不等例照旧：cert_eff = 0 时轨迹条件为 0（T23c）", j0["轨迹"] == 0)
    pulse_m = dict(pulse_x, 量=F(1, 2), 拍=-1)
    proc_m = {"类": "过程", "名": "p", "拍": 0, "目标": "x", "载体在内": True, "律": "linear", "读取": ("x",),
              "系数": F(1), "偏置": F(0)}
    jo = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m], closed_graph=False)
    jc = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m], closed_graph=True)
    jl = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m], [("state:x:0", "p", 1)], closed_graph=False)
    jun = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m])
    check("T23n 闭图条件由证据协议申报：无路径的开图 HDyn_F = %s，闭图 %s，有认证路径 %s；未申报时 %s" %
          (jo["HDyn_F"], jc["HDyn_F"], jl["HDyn_F"], jun["D20"]),
          jo["HDyn_F"] == "⊥" and jc["HDyn_F"] == 0 and jl["HDyn_F"] == 1 and jun["D20"].startswith("拒绝"))
    # ---------------- T24 律层的实际生效与差转移（定理 18-8） ----------------
    print("T24　律层的实际生效与差转移（定理 18-8（二）—（五））")
    _, h1b, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H1, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    _, h2b, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H2, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    check("T24a 门控不入账：ε_L = 1 的有效入账下 0→1 的巩固分项不入账，强写入拍后 c 差仍在（c(0) = %s 对 %s），覆盖不发生；原机制下同拍覆盖（T15d）" %
          (fs(h1b[("c", 0)]), fs(h2b[("c", 0)])), h1b[("c", 0)] != h2b[("c", 0)] and h1n == h2n)
    htr = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"read_z": d["z"]},
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("清空", -d["x"])], "z": [("转移", d["x"])]})
    t1 = C.run(htr, {"x": F(1, 2), "z": F(0)}, None, ("s", "s"))
    t2 = C.run(htr, {"x": F(1, 4), "z": F(0)}, None, ("s", "s"))
    check("T24b 差转移：两臂 x 深度 1/2 与 1/4，一拍后 x 都为 0，差转到 z；第二拍读 z 得 %s 对 %s：可读差不要求原位差存续" %
          (fs(t1[0][1]["read_z"]), fs(t2[0][1]["read_z"])),
          t1[1]["x"] == t2[1]["x"] == 0 and t1[0][1] != t2[0][1])
    _, wk1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, G1, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    _, wk2, _, _ = C.step(flyc, G2, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    check("T24c 弱写入在 ε_L = 1 的有效入账下不入账：两臂一拍后 α3 深度同为 %s，（五）的实际后态差前件不成立" % fs(wk1[(0, "a3")]),
          wk1[(0, "a3")] == wk2[(0, "a3")] == 0)
    hl = C.Host(["x", "c"], lambda d, r, s: {"r": F(1)},
                lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - (F(1, 2) if d["c"] == 0 else F(9, 10))) * d["x"])], "c": [("set", 1 - d["c"])]})
    pl = [C.step(hl, {"x": F(0), "c": F(cc)}, None, "s") for cc in (0, 1)]
    check("T24d 全事件首次差可先落在 c：两臂 x = 0、c = 0 与 1，强门同拍置位；弛豫分项同为 %s，置位分项 %s 对 %s" %
          (fs(pl[0][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1]), fs(pl[0][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1]), fs(pl[1][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1])),
          pl[0][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == pl[1][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == 0 and pl[0][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1] != pl[1][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1])
    def halfpost(dn, do):
        return {k_: v_ / 2 for k_, v_ in dn.items()}
    arms_h = []
    for rho_ in (F(1, 2), F(9, 10)):
        hx = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda rr: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - rr) * d["x"])]}))(rho_), halfpost)
        arms_h.append(C.step(hx, {"x": F(1, 2)}, None, "s")[1]["x"])
    comp_diff = (F(9, 10) - F(1, 2)) * F(1, 2)
    check("T24e 逐位 post(v)_x = v_x/2（不截断、不取格）：分项差 %s，实际后态差 %s（减半）；实际后态差须按两臂实际后处理后的值计" %
          (fs(comp_diff), fs(arms_h[1] - arms_h[0])), arms_h[1] - arms_h[0] == comp_diff / 2)
    hr0 = C.Host(["x", "c"], lambda d, r, s: r, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - (F(1, 2) if d["c"] == 0 else F(9, 10))) * d["x"])]})
    pr0 = [C.step(hr0, {"x": F(0), "c": F(cc)}, F(cc), "R") for cc in (0, 1)]
    check("T24f 排除例：同 d 异 r（d_x = 0、c 为 0 与 1、初始 r 为 0 与 1、Φ = r），首拍读数即为 %s 对 %s，弛豫分项同为 0——定理 18-8 须两臂除 c 外初始完整状态（含 r）相同" %
          (fs(pr0[0][0]), fs(pr0[1][0])), pr0[0][0] != pr0[1][0] and pr0[0][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == pr0[1][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == 0)
    hr1 = C.Host(["x", "c"], lambda d, r, s: {"y": d["x"] + r},
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - (F(1, 2) if d["c"] == 0 else F(9, 10))) * d["x"])]},
                 None, lambda r, y: r / 2 + y["y"] / 4)
    ra = C.run(hr1, {"x": F(1, 2), "c": F(0)}, F(1, 2), ("R", "R"))[0]
    rb2 = C.run(hr1, {"x": F(1, 2), "c": F(1)}, F(1, 2), ("R", "R"))[0]
    check("T24g 正例：初始残留相同（r = 1/2）、残留递推 P(r, y) = r/2 + y/4 两臂相同而不读 c，首拍读数同为 %s；首次目标深度弛豫分项差之后第二拍读数 %s 对 %s" %
          (fs(ra[0]["y"]), fs(ra[1]["y"]), fs(rb2[1]["y"])), ra[0] == rb2[0] and ra[1] != rb2[1])
    # ---------------- T25 证明见证补充（定理 18-1（二）、18-2（三）、18-7（五）） ----------------
    print("T25　证明见证补充（定理 18-1（二）、18-2（三）、18-7（五））")
    check("T25a O 不同而 Φ 相同：同 d 异 c（Φ 不读 c）的两态在一切信号下全活动相同（T8a 两态），而带标签组织描述不同",
          P0 != P1 and all(M.flyc_read(P0, None, x_) == M.flyc_read(P1, None, x_) for x_ in "ABCR"))
    check("T25b 固定实际信号下 Φ 不同而 y 相同：两管网态在实际注入下全活动相同、在结点 2 探针下不同（T1d 两态）",
          QXa == QYa and pXa == pYa and pXb[2] != pYb[2])
    ms1 = sorted(M.fly_read(Xs, None, "A").values())
    ms_all = {x_: sorted(M.fly_read(Xt, None, x_).values()) for x_ in "ABC"}
    check("T25c γ1↔α3 互换下读出交换式对任一信号置换 σ_g 与活动坐标置换 ψ 都失效：Φ(d, A) 的值多重集 %s 与 Φ(π·d, s′) 的值多重集（s′ = A、B、C）都不同" %
          [fs(v) for v in ms1], all(ms1 != m_ for m_ in ms_all.values()))
    rN, rP = F(4, 5), F(0)
    yy = [2 * (F(1, 2) * rN ** n_ - F(1, 2) * rP ** n_) for n_ in range(1, 6)]
    check("T25d 平行对立中一支退浅率为 0：差式 y(n) = 2d(r_N^n − r_P^n) 对 n ≥ 1 恒不为 0（不用比值）", all(v != 0 for v in yy))
    print("=" * 60)
    nb = RES.count(False)
    print("合计 %d 项，通过 %d 项，不符 %d 项" % (len(RES), len(RES) - nb, nb))
    return 1 if nb else 0

def ETA_A():
    return M.ETA * M.A_INN

def ETA_B():
    return M.ETA * M.B_DIS

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
~~~

A.4　field\_final.py　〔封二〕《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1 的 F 层求值器（D10—D20），与该附录逐字相同

~~~python
"""场具化数理式 v1.0 附录 A：F 层（有限可分层）求值器。
形式模拟，不是物质因果认证。
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """输入数值按其十进制表示取精确有理数；F 层全部运算在有理数上精确进行。"""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # 合法数值：整数、有理数（Fraction）或有限的十进制浮点数
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # certified 在 F 中是脉冲「是否实际发生」的认证：1 发生，0 不发生；⊥ 不属 F 单轨迹定义域，须在 R 中按分支枚举
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20：作用认证须已决定；作用未定的机制不属 F 定义域（零分项亦然）
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # 界外成因：已认证、有效的界外作用分项（脉冲或界外载体过程）在该位留下的改变
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # 只计已认证发生且有效的脉冲；确认不发生者不计贡献、不登记形成与成因
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # 组织条件的支持：数值律依赖读取位，或纯读取过程（分项为零）；读取认证另作合取，未定不预先截为假
            # 函数支持按数学上的非零偏导判定（系数与伙伴读数精确不为零），不与有效改变阈值 ε 混用
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # 新元素的形成：B 内载体者为场内续行形成，界外载体者为信号形成
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # 界外载体过程的有效分项，不论新旧元素、不论正负，都是信号留下的改变
                if not m.carrier_inside:
~~~
